Astrology name feasibility audit · v81

Twelve panels give ten surviving sign identities. They do not give ten identified Voynich name tokens. No sounds fitted.

What earlier work permits

v65 atypical spelling is a model-relative candidate signal, not evidence of phonetic writing. v79/v80 supplied correct name pairs and boundaries; v80 first passed at 36 names for full letters and consonant-only in that benchmark. Ten signs cannot inherit that qualification, nor does 36 establish a universal requirement. f9v remains excluded from fitting.

Coverage and referents

No F transcription is present here. All available readings and uncertain slots are preserved. The later readable month annotations cannot supply Voynich glyph sounds. Most labels attach to figures/stars, possibly degree or calendar designations. Source comments call f70v2.C a central star, not the sign title.

Local Davis-derived metadata assigns f73r/v hand3, whereas current voynich.nu descriptions list hand4. Conflict preserved; no pooling or inference resolving it.

Historically attested written candidates

One edited c1391 tradition, not a complete multilingual vocabulary or phonological reconstruction. Capricorn/Aquarius are source controls; their Voynich zodiac panels do not survive.

SignWritten forms
AriesAries
TaurusTaurus
GeminiGemini
CancerCancer, Cancre
LeoLeo
VirgoVirgo
LibraLibra
ScorpioScorpio, Scorpioun
SagittariusSagittarius, Sagittarie
CapricornCapricornus, Capricorne, Capricorn
AquariusAquarius, Aquarie
PiscesPisces

Productive tests

Register the exact image-to-label relation first. Repeated Aries and Taurus can test same-name consistency; shared words are currently descriptive only. Keep duplicate panels in one sign-level training/test group. Compare candidate outliers with same-hand, same-format peers, not body prose. Freeze all language, spelling, segmentation, vowel-omission and slot alternatives before evaluating a common mapping. Test wrong sign assignments with the entire selection/search procedure repeated. Require predictions for signs withheld as whole groups. A failure with ten signs may be low coverage rather than evidence against phonetic naming.

f69v has a published 28-lunar-mansion interpretation, but it remains conditional; count/order/name alignment must not be assumed. Stars, planets and the proposed f68r3 Pleiades need their own referent audits.

Complete source loci

Every locus is included; no attractive matches selected. Open each row for all readings.
